# Claim ledger: terminal levels 1 to 3, audit round 2 (terminal-1-3-r2)

Stage 0 depth: one row for every graded key, wrong option, slip, prose rule, explain line, summary line and command claim, at commit 70b1dc4f.
Written blind, before any earlier read was opened. "run" means the claim was executed: bash 5.2.21, zsh 5.9, GNU coreutils and grep on Linux,
CPython 3.11 to 3.13, and Pyodide 0.27.7 for the code widgets. Mac-only wording (BSD mkdir, rm, wc, Finder) is cited, not run: no macOS here.
Script: `docs/reports/terminal-L1-3-concepts-r2.py`.

Rows: 1281. Verdicts: correct 1271, wrong 0, imprecise 4, misleading 6, unverified 0.
